# Introduction

As a support technician, you play a crucial role in ensuring the smooth operation of computer systems and providing efficient technical support to end users. This chapter focuses on operational procedures. A vital aspect of your role involves following established protocols and best practices to maintain a productive and secure computing environment.

In this chapter, you will face questions on the key objectives outlined in CompTIA A+ Core 2 (220-1102) domain 4: Operational Procedures. These objectives are designed to equip you with the necessary knowledge and skills to perform your duties effectively and contribute to the overall success of your organization.

By mastering these operational procedures, you will enhance your effectiveness as a helpdesk technician and improve user satisfaction.

In order to be successful on the CompTIA A+ Core 2 (220-1102) certification exam, candidates must ensure they are familiar with the following objectives:

*   10.1 Given a scenario, implement best practices associated with documentation and support systems information management
*   10.2 Explain basic change-management best practices
*   10.3 Given a scenario, implement workstation backup and recovery methods
*   10.4 Given a scenario, use common safety procedures
*   10.5 Summarize environmental impacts and local environmental controls
*   10.6 Explain the importance of prohibited content/activity and privacy, licensing, and policy concepts
*   10.7 Given a scenario, use proper communication techniques and professionalism
*   10.8 Identify the basics of scripting
*   10.9 Given a scenario, use remote access technologies

The rest of this chapter is dedicated to practice. For each of the concepts defined above, you will be given a series of questions designed to test your knowledge of each core objective as defined by the official certification exam guidance for this domain. These questions will assess the candidate’s knowledge of how to implement operational procedures.

# Practice Exam Questions

## 10.1 Given a scenario, implement best practices associated with documentation and support systems information management

1.  A company is implementing regulatory compliance requirements for its documentation and support systems. It wants to ensure that users are informed about the organization’s policies and procedures before accessing the system. Which of the following best practices should it implement? 
    1.  Password complexity rules
    2.  Network segmentation
    3.  Splash screens
    4.  Incident reports
2.  A helpdesk team is responsible for documenting and reporting any incidents that occur during their support activities. Which of the following documents should they use to record detailed information about each incident? 
    1.  Knowledge base articles
    2.  Splash screens
    3.  Incident reports
    4.  Service-level agreements
3.  Which of the following is an example of a best practice for complying with regulatory requirements related to incident reporting? 
    1.  Implementing splash screens on all company devices
    2.  Conducting regular vulnerability assessments
    3.  Encrypting sensitive customer data during transit
    4.  Training employees on data handling and privacy policies
4.  Which of the following is an example of documentation that should be included in a support system information management plan? 
    1.  Network diagrams
    2.  Software licenses
    3.  Customer contact information
    4.  User passwords
5.  In the context of support system information management, which of the following is the purpose of a knowledge base? 
    1.  To store customer feedback
    2.  To track hardware inventory
    3.  To centralize troubleshooting guides
    4.  To schedule technician appointments
6.  When documenting a support request, which of the following details should be included? 
    1.  Customer’s favorite color
    2.  Date and time of the request
    3.  Technician’s lunch preferences
    4.  Weather conditions in the area
7.  Which of the following is the best practice for managing support system documentation? 
    1.  Storing documents in multiple physical locations
    2.  Sharing sensitive information via email
    3.  Regularly updating and reviewing documentation
    4.  Printing documentation only when necessary
8.  Which of the following is an example of a ticketing system used in support systems? 
    1.  Microsoft Office Suite
    2.  ServiceNow
    3.  Adobe Photoshop
    4.  Google Chrome
9.  What is the primary purpose of a service level agreement (SLA) in support system documentation? 
    1.  To outline the technician’s work schedule
    2.  To establish pricing for support services
    3.  To define response and resolution times
    4.  To determine the customer’s preferred contact method
10.  Which of the following should be included in a support system knowledge base article? 
     1.  Sales promotional material
     2.  Personal opinions of support technicians
     3.  Tested solutions to known issues
     4.  Company’s financial reports
11.  Which of the following is a common method of organizing and categorizing support system documentation? 
     1.  Alphabetical order
     2.  Reverse chronological order
     3.  Color-coded labels
     4.  Random arrangement
12.  Which of the following is an example of a customer relationship management (CRM) system used in support systems? 
     1.  Salesforce
     2.  Dropbox
     3.  Microsoft Word
     4.  Spotify
13.  In support system documentation, what is the purpose of creating an inventory list? 
     1.  To track technician attendance records
     2.  To monitor customer satisfaction ratings
     3.  To keep an inventory of spare parts and equipment
     4.  To schedule routine maintenance tasks
14.  Which of the following statements best describes an acceptable use policy (AUP) in relation to documentation and support systems information management? 
     1.  An AUP outlines the procedures for creating backups of critical data
     2.  An AUP establishes guidelines for the appropriate use of computer systems and networks
     3.  An AUP specifies the steps to troubleshoot and resolve hardware-related issues
     4.  An AUP defines the process for documenting software license agreements

## 10.2 Explain basic change-management best practices

1.  A company is updating operational procedures and will adopt change management. Which of the following best describes a basic change-management best practice? 
    1.  Implementing changes without proper planning
    2.  Communicating changes only after they have been implemented
    3.  Documenting changes thoroughly
    4.  Ignoring user feedback
2.  Which of the following is a key element of change management? 
    1.  Minimizing user involvement
    2.  Avoiding communication with stakeholders
    3.  Assessing and managing risks
    4.  Rushing the implementation process
3.  Which of the following is a recommended practice for managing user resistance during change implementation? 
    1.  Ignoring user concerns
    2.  Providing clear communication and training
    3.  Limiting user access to system documentation
    4.  Implementing changes abruptly without prior notice
4.  Which of the following best describes the purpose of a change advisory board (CAB)? 
    1.  Approving all changes without review
    2.  Monitoring the progress of change implementation
    3.  Evaluating and prioritizing proposed changes
    4.  Limiting user involvement in the change process
5.  Which of the following is a common risk associated with change management? 
    1.  Improved system performance
    2.  Increased user satisfaction
    3.  Temporary service disruptions
    4.  Enhanced security measures
6.  Which of the following is an example of a change management best practice for software deployments? 
    1.  Skipping the testing phase
    2.  Performing regular backups
    3.  Implementing changes during peak hours
    4.  Avoiding user communication
7.  Which of the following is an example of a change management best practice for hardware replacements? 
    1.  Ignoring user feedback
    2.  Conducting a thorough inventory assessment
    3.  Implementing changes without testing
    4.  Excluding stakeholders from the process
8.  Which of the following best describes the purpose of a rollback plan in change management? 
    1.  Preventing any changes from being implemented
    2.  Documenting the reasons for change implementation
    3.  Identifying potential issues and their resolutions
    4.  Excluding user feedback during the change process
9.  Which of the following is a recommended practice for managing change-related risks? 
    1.  Avoiding risk assessment altogether
    2.  Implementing changes without backup plans
    3.  Conducting regular risk assessments
    4.  Ignoring risk mitigation strategies
10.  Which of the following is a key consideration when communicating changes to users during change management? 
     1.  Providing vague and general information
     2.  Overemphasizing the negative aspects of the changes
     3.  Tailoring the message to the target audience
     4.  Delaying communication until after the changes are implemented

## 10.3 Given a scenario, implement workstation backup and recovery methods

1.  Which workstation backup method involves creating a copy of all user data and settings at a specific point in time? 
    1.  Full backup
    2.  Differential backup
    3.  Incremental backup
    4.  Selective backup
2.  Which workstation recovery method involves using a backup of the entire workstation’s hard drive to restore the system to its previous state? 
    1.  Bare-metal recovery
    2.  Cloud-based recovery
    3.  File-level recovery
    4.  Incremental recovery
3.  Which backup storage medium provides the fastest transfer rates but is also the most expensive option for workstation backup? 
    1.  Tape drive
    2.  External hard drive
    3.  Network-attached storage (NAS)
    4.  Cloud storage
4.  Which workstation backup method only backs up data that has changed since the last backup, regardless of whether it was a full or incremental backup? 
    1.  Differential backup
    2.  Incremental backup
    3.  Synthetic backup
    4.  Continuous backup
5.  Which backup rotation scheme involves using a set of backup media and cycling through them on a daily basis, overwriting the oldest backup when the set is full? 
    1.  Grandfather-father-son
    2.  Tower of Hanoi
    3.  Back up to disk
    4.  Back up to tape
6.  Which workstation backup method provides the fastest recovery time when compared to other backup methods? 
    1.  Incremental backup
    2.  Differential backup
    3.  Snapshot backup
    4.  Full backup
7.  Which backup method ensures data redundancy by creating multiple copies of backups on different backup media? 
    1.  Mirroring
    2.  Replication
    3.  Archiving
    4.  Versioning
8.  Which backup method provides the highest level of data protection by storing multiple copies of backups across different geographical locations? 
    1.  Cloud-based backup
    2.  Tape backup
    3.  Disk-to-disk backup
    4.  Offsite backup
9.  Which workstation backup method captures an exact copy of the workstation’s operating system, applications, and data, allowing for quick restoration to a functional state? 
    1.  Image backup
    2.  File-level backup
    3.  Incremental backup
    4.  Differential backup
10.  Which workstation backup method involves automatically backing up data in real time as changes occur, ensuring minimal data loss in the event of a failure? 
     1.  Continuous backup
     2.  Synchronization backup
     3.  Manual backup
     4.  Snapshot backup

## 10.4 Given a scenario, use common safety procedures

1.  Which of the following is a common safety procedure when working on computer hardware? 
    1.  Wear an ESD wrist strap
    2.  Disconnect the power supply without turning it off
    3.  Handle circuit boards with bare hands
    4.  Stand on a wet surface
2.  When working on computer equipment, which of the following safety precautions should be taken? 
    1.  Use compressed air to clean debris from fans and heatsinks
    2.  Use a magnetized screwdriver to secure components
    3.  Store liquids near the computer to easily access them
    4.  Place the computer on an unstable surface
3.  Which of the following safety measures should be followed when handling computer cables? 
    1.  Pull the cable from a distance to disconnect it
    2.  Bend the cable sharply to fit it in tight spaces
    3.  Secure cables using cable ties or clips
    4.  Leave cables loose and tangled
4.  What safety precaution should be taken before opening a computer case? 
    1.  Ensure the computer is turned off and unplugged
    2.  Wear gloves to protect against static electricity
    3.  Use a metal tool to open the case
    4.  Keep the case on a soft, cushioned surface
5.  What should be done to prevent injuries while working with computer hardware? 
    1.  Follow ergonomic practices
    2.  Wear heavy jewelry and accessories
    3.  Use damaged tools if no others are available
    4.  Skip breaks and work for long periods without rest
6.  Which safety measure should be taken when cleaning the inside of a computer? 
    1.  Use an antistatic vacuum cleaner
    2.  Spray cleaning solution directly onto components
    3.  Clean the computer while it is still powered on
    4.  Use a dry cloth to wipe the motherboard
7.  Which safety precaution should be taken when disposing of old computer equipment? 
    1.  Recycle the equipment according to local regulations
    2.  Disassemble the equipment without protective gear
    3.  Throw the equipment in regular household waste
    4.  Store the equipment in a humid environment
8.  Which of the following safety precautions should be followed when working with high-voltage equipment? 
    1.  Wear insulated gloves and safety glasses
    2.  Work alone to minimize distractions
    3.  Use a metal ladder when working at heights
    4.  Use equipment with damaged cords
9.  What should be done if a chemical spill occurs in the vicinity of computer equipment? 
    1.  Shut down the equipment immediately
    2.  Use a vacuum cleaner to remove the spill
    3.  Follow proper procedures for chemical cleanup
    4.  Ignore the spill and continue working
10.  What safety measures should be taken when installing or replacing computer hardware components? 
     1.  Use proper lifting techniques for heavy components
     2.  Use excessive force when inserting connectors
     3.  Install components in an unventilated area
     4.  Install components without grounding yourself

## 10.5 Summarize environmental impacts and local environmental controls

1.  Which of the following describes the purpose of material safety data sheets (MSDSs) in relation to handling and disposal of hazardous materials? 
    1.  To provide guidelines for safe transportation of hazardous materials
    2.  To document the potential hazards and precautions for using a specific material
    3.  To outline the proper procedures for recycling electronic waste
    4.  To certify the quality and durability of protective equipment
2.  What is the primary purpose of reviewing material safety data sheets (MSDSs) before handling or disposing of hazardous materials? 
    1.  To assess the financial cost associated with the materials
    2.  To determine the expiration date of the materials
    3.  To understand the potential health and safety risks
    4.  To identify potential sources of the materials
3.  Proper battery disposal is essential to protect the environment and prevent hazardous materials from contaminating landfills. Which of the following statements accurately describes the correct way to dispose of used batteries? 
    1.  Place used batteries in the regular trash bin
    2.  Take the batteries to a nearby electronics store for disposal
    3.  Recycle used batteries at designated recycling centers or facilities
    4.  Bury the batteries in the backyard to decompose naturally
4.  When disposing of toner cartridges from printers and copiers, what is the recommended method to ensure proper disposal? 
    1.  Place the toner cartridges in any nearby recycling bin for regular waste collection
    2.  Throw the toner cartridges in the trash as they are not considered hazardous waste
    3.  Return the used toner cartridges to the manufacturer or a designated recycling program
    4.  Burn the toner cartridges to reduce waste volume before disposing of them
5.  When disposing of other electronic devices and assets, which of the following methods should you avoid to ensure data security and prevent environmental damage? (Choose all that apply.) 
    1.  Selling the devices to an unknown individual or entity
    2.  Donating the devices to a local charity or non-profit organization
    3.  Erasing all the data from the devices before recycling or disposal
    4.  Disassembling the devices and disposing of their parts separately
6.  What environmental factors should be considered to maintain optimal equipment performance and longevity? 
    1.  Temperature, humidity, and proper ventilation
    2.  Noise level, cable management, and power supply
    3.  Lighting conditions, firewall settings, and antivirus software
    4.  Backup systems, network security, and data encryption
7.  What device is designed to protect electronic equipment from power surges, under-voltage events, and power failures? 
    1.  Uninterruptible Power Supply (UPS)
    2.  Power over Ethernet (PoE)
    3.  Network-Attached Storage (NAS)
    4.  Wireless Access Point (WAP)
8.  Which device is used to suppress power surges and voltage spikes? 
    1.  Surge protector
    2.  Uninterruptible Power Supply (UPS)
    3.  Power inverter
    4.  Power strip
9.  What is the purpose of using a battery backup system? 
    1.  To provide backup power during outages
    2.  To increase network bandwidth
    3.  To protect against electromagnetic interference
    4.  To prevent power surges
10.  What is a recommended practice for location/equipment placement to ensure optimal computer performance? 
     1.  Placing the computer on an uneven surface
     2.  Locating the computer near direct sunlight
     3.  Positioning the computer away from electrical interference
     4.  Stacking multiple computers on top of each other
11.  A technician must choose the best method suitable for dust cleanup in a computer system. Which of these is the recommended solution? 
     1.  Blowing compressed air directly into the components
     2.  Using a dry cloth to wipe the components
     3.  Applying water or liquid cleaning agents on the components
     4.  Utilizing a vacuum designed for electronics

## 10.6 Explain the importance of prohibited content/activity and privacy, licensing, and policy concepts

1.  Which concept ensures the integrity and preservation of data during an incident response in relation to prohibited content/activity? 
    1.  Chain of custody
    2.  Incident escalation
    3.  Password recovery
    4.  Data compression
2.  When encountering prohibited content/activity during an incident response, which action should be taken? 
    1.  Inform management or law enforcement, if necessary
    2.  Continue the investigation without involving anyone else
    3.  Delete the prohibited content/activity immediately
    4.  Perform a system reboot
3.  What is the purpose of making a copy of a drive during an incident response? 
    1.  To preserve data integrity and evidence
    2.  To speed up the incident resolution process
    3.  To minimize the impact on system performance
    4.  To reduce the risk of future incidents
4.  Which concept involves documenting the handling and transfer of evidence during an incident response? 
    1.  Chain of custody
    2.  Incident containment
    3.  Data classification
    4.  System hardening
5.  What is the purpose of documenting an incident during an incident response? 
    1.  To provide a reference for future incidents
    2.  To inform management of ongoing activities
    3.  To facilitate collaboration with other teams
    4.  To create a detailed record for analysis and accountability
6.  Which concept defines the terms and conditions under which software can be used by an individual or organization? 
    1.  Privacy policy
    2.  Open source license
    3.  End-user license agreement (EULA)
    4.  Digital rights management (DRM)
7.  What distinguishes a personal use license from a corporate use license? 
    1.  Personal use licenses require individual registration, while corporate use licenses allow multiple users
    2.  Personal use licenses have shorter validity periods compared to corporate use licenses
    3.  Personal use licenses have fewer restrictions on usage compared to corporate use licenses
    4.  Personal use licenses are free, while corporate use licenses require a fee
8.  Which licensing concept ensures that users comply with the terms of the software license and prevents unauthorized copying or distribution? 
    1.  Open source license
    2.  Valid license
    3.  Non-expired license
    4.  Digital rights management (DRM)
9.  What is the purpose of licensing/digital rights management (DRM)/the end-user license agreement (EULA) in relation to prohibited content/activity? 
    1.  They facilitate the sharing of prohibited content/activity
    2.  They prevent the use of prohibited content/activity
    3.  They monitor and report the use of prohibited content/activity
    4.  They establish penalties for the use of prohibited content/activity
10.  Which type of license allows users to view, modify, and distribute the source code of software? 
     1.  Privacy policy
     2.  Open source license
     3.  End-user license agreement (EULA)
     4.  Digital rights management (DRM)
11.  Which of the following best describes regulated data in the context of privacy and policy concepts? 
     1.  Data that is subject to government regulations and restrictions
     2.  Data that is freely accessible to the public
     3.  Data that is stored without any encryption or security measures
     4.  Data that is shared among employees within an organization
12.  Which type of data is considered as personally identifiable information (PII)? 
     1.  Social media posts and comments
     2.  Names and addresses of individuals
     3.  Publicly available company information
     4.  Non-sensitive email communication
13.  Which of the following describes a key concern when handling credit card transactions? 
     1.  Ensuring data availability
     2.  Securing cardholder data
     3.  Maintaining accurate financial records
     4.  Maximizing transaction speed
14.  Which of the following is an example of personal government-issued information? 
     1.  Social media account usernames
     2.  Driver’s license number
     3.  Wi-Fi network passwords
     4.  Home address
15.  Which of the following best describes data retention requirements? 
     1.  The amount of time it takes to process data
     2.  The policies and procedures for storing and managing data
     3.  The frequency at which data is backed up
     4.  The speed of data transmission over a network

## 10.7 Given a scenario, use proper communication techniques and professionalism

1.  When service desk personnel are communicating with customers, which communication technique demonstrates professionalism and empathy? 
    1.  Interrupting the customer to provide quick solutions
    2.  Using technical jargon to establish expertise
    3.  Active listening and paraphrasing the customer’s concerns
    4.  Offering discounts or freebies to resolve the issue
2.  Which of the following is an example of non-verbal communication that reflects professionalism? 
    1.  Maintaining eye contact and nodding in agreement
    2.  Checking personal emails on a mobile device
    3.  Speaking softly to avoid disrupting others
    4.  Using slang terms and informal language
3.  In a business email, which of the following is the most appropriate way to address the recipient? 
    1.  Hey \[Recipient’s First Name\],
    2.  Dear \[Recipient’s First Name\],
    3.  To whom it may concern,
    4.  Hi there,
4.  Which of the following communication methods is most suitable for conveying complex technical information to a non-technical audience? 
    1.  Using technical terms and acronyms extensively
    2.  Providing detailed written documentation only
    3.  Utilizing visual aids, diagrams, and illustrations
    4.  Speaking rapidly to ensure all information is covered
5.  Which of the following is an example of appropriate use of professional language in customer communication? 
    1.  “Your computer is totally messed up”
    2.  “Your device is experiencing some technical difficulties”
    3.  “Your machine is acting weird”
    4.  “Your PC is broken”
6.  When speaking with a customer, which of the following is a professional way to handle a disagreement or conflict? 
    1.  Raising your voice to assert dominance
    2.  Blaming the customer for the issue
    3.  Offering alternative solutions and compromises
    4.  Ignoring the customer’s concerns
7.  Which of the following is an example of appropriate professional behavior when interacting with colleagues? 
    1.  Gossiping about other team members
    2.  Taking credit for others’ work
    3.  Actively listening and respecting others’ opinions
    4.  Criticizing colleagues in public forums
8.  Which of the following communication methods is best suited for delivering sensitive or confidential information? 
    1.  Public announcement during a team meeting
    2.  Sending a mass email to all employees
    3.  Holding individual face-to-face meetings
    4.  Posting the information on a public bulletin board
9.  Which of the following is an example of maintaining professionalism in a virtual meeting? 
    1.  Eating a meal while on camera
    2.  Keeping the microphone on mute when not speaking
    3.  Using informal language and abbreviations in chat messages
    4.  Sharing irrelevant personal anecdotes
10.  When communicating with customers, which of the following is a professional approach to managing their expectations? 
     1.  Overpromising to ensure customer satisfaction
     2.  Setting realistic expectations and providing updates
     3.  Ignoring customer requests to avoid conflicts
     4.  Redirecting customers to a different support channel
11.  Which of the following actions should a technician take to deal appropriately with a customer’s confidential and private materials stored on a desktop computer? 
     1.  Regularly share the customer’s data with colleagues for collaborative purposes
     2.  Encrypt the customer’s sensitive files and folders using strong encryption algorithms
     3.  Upload the customer’s confidential data to a cloud storage service for backup
     4.  Dispose of the customer’s computer without securely wiping the hard drive
12.  When dealing with a customer’s confidential materials on a printer, which of the following steps should a technician take to maintain security? 
     1.  Share the printer’s access credentials with other technicians for easier troubleshooting
     2.  Disable logging features on the printer to prevent potential privacy breaches
     3.  Implement access controls on the printer to restrict unauthorized usage
     4.  Print the customer’s confidential documents in a public area for quick access
13.  A technician is helping a customer secure their desktop computer. The technician must implement best practices to protect the customer’s confidential materials. Which offers the best solution? 
     1.  Create a shared user account with unrestricted access for the customer and their colleagues
     2.  Regularly perform data backups without verifying the integrity and confidentiality of the backup media
     3.  Implement strong password policies and enable full disk encryption for the customer’s desktop
     4.  Store the customer’s confidential files in publicly accessible folders for easier collaboration

## 10.8 Identify the basics of scripting

1.  A developer must identify the scripting language used by the web development community. Which scripting language will be the best choice? 
    1.  Python
    2.  Bash
    3.  JavaScript
    4.  PowerShell
2.  Which scripting language is commonly used for task automation in Windows environments? 
    1.  Python
    2.  Bash
    3.  JavaScript
    4.  PowerShell
3.  Which scripting language is commonly used for system administration tasks in Unix-like operating systems? 
    1.  Python
    2.  Bash
    3.  JavaScript
    4.  PowerShell
4.  Which scripting language is known for its versatility and can be used for a wide range of tasks, including web development, automation, and data analysis? 
    1.  Python
    2.  Bash
    3.  JavaScript
    4.  PowerShell
5.  Which scripting language is commonly used for client-side scripting in web browsers? 
    1.  Python
    2.  Bash
    3.  JavaScript
    4.  PowerShell
6.  What are some potential risks associated with using scripts that need to be considered to avoid unintentionally introducing malware, inadvertently changing system settings, and causing browser or system crashes due to mishandling of resources? 
    1.  Inadequate error handling
    2.  Insufficient debugging tools
    3.  Lack of version control
    4.  Incompatible scripting languages
7.  Which precaution should be taken to prevent unintentionally introducing malware when using scripts? 
    1.  Regularly update antivirus software
    2.  Implement input validation and sanitization
    3.  Disable the firewall temporarily
    4.  Increase user privileges
8.  What can cause browser or system crashes due to mishandling of resources when using scripts? 
    1.  Excessive memory usage
    2.  Frequent disk read/write operations
    3.  Incompatible browser extensions
    4.  Network connectivity issues
9.  Which of the following actions poses a risk of unintentionally introducing malware into a system when using scripts? 
    1.  Using reputable script repositories
    2.  Regularly updating the script interpreter
    3.  Downloading scripts from untrusted sources
    4.  Running scripts in a sandbox environment
10.  What can be a consequence of inadvertently changing system settings when executing scripts on a computer? 
     1.  Improved system performance
     2.  Enhanced user experience
     3.  Increased security measures
     4.  System instability or malfunction
11.  To prevent browser or system crashes due to mishandling of resources in scripts, what should be done? 
     1.  Increase script execution speed
     2.  Avoid error handling in scripts
     3.  Optimize resource usage in scripts
     4.  Disable browser extensions

## 10.9 Given a scenario, use remote access technologies

1.  A technician needs to troubleshoot a user’s computer remotely. The user is running a Windows operating system and is connected to the same network as the technician. Which remote access technology should the technician use in this scenario? 
    1.  Microsoft Remote Assistance (MSRA)
    2.  Virtual Network Computing (VNC)
    3.  Remote Desktop Protocol (RDP)
    4.  Secure Shell (SSH)
2.  In a scenario where an IT technician needs to remotely monitor and manage multiple devices in a network, which remote access technology would be most suitable? 
    1.  Virtual private network (VPN)
    2.  Remote Desktop Protocol (RDP)
    3.  Telnet
    4.  Remote monitoring and management (RMM)
3.  Which remote access technology allows users to access their office computer from a remote location? 
    1.  Virtual private network (VPN)
    2.  Remote Desktop Protocol (RDP)
    3.  Telnet
    4.  Secure Shell (SSH)
4.  In a scenario where a user needs to remotely access a Windows server, which remote access technology would be most suitable? 
    1.  Remote Desktop Protocol (RDP)
    2.  File Transfer Protocol (FTP)
    3.  Telnet
    4.  Secure Shell (SSH)
5.  A user wants to remotely connect to a Linux server using a command-line interface. Which remote access technology would be most appropriate? 
    1.  Telnet
    2.  Remote Desktop Protocol (RDP)
    3.  Secure Shell (SSH)
    4.  Virtual private network (VPN)
6.  In a scenario where a user wants to remotely access their home network from a remote network, which remote access technology would be the most appropriate? 
    1.  Virtual private network (VPN)
    2.  Remote Desktop Protocol (RDP)
    3.  Telnet
    4.  Secure Shell (SSH)
7.  Which remote access technology commonly uses port 3389 by default? 
    1.  Remote Desktop Protocol (RDP)
    2.  File Transfer Protocol (FTP)
    3.  Telnet
    4.  Secure Shell (SSH)
8.  In a scenario where a user wants to remotely access a Mac computer from another Mac, which built-in remote access technology can be used? 
    1.  Apple Remote Desktop
    2.  Virtual Network Computing (VNC)
    3.  Remote Desktop Protocol (RDP)
    4.  Secure Shell (SSH)
9.  Which remote access technology allows users to access their office network resources securely over the internet? 
    1.  Virtual private network (VPN)
    2.  Remote Desktop Protocol (RDP)
    3.  Telnet
    4.  Secure Shell (SSH)

# 11

# Mock Exam: Core 2 (220-1102)

The final chapter is intended to provide the candidate with a realistic mock exam comprising 93 questions. The official CompTIA A+ Core 2 (220-1102) certification exam allows for 90 minutes to complete all questions. This mock exam is intended to cover all the following CompTIA certification objectives:

*   Install, configure, and maintain computer equipment, mobile devices, and software for end users
*   Service components based on customer requirements
*   Understand networking basics and apply basic cybersecurity methods to mitigate threats
*   Properly and safely diagnose, resolve, and document common hardware and software issues
*   Apply troubleshooting skills and provide customer support using appropriate communication skills
*   Understand the basics of scripting, cloud technologies, virtualization, and multi-OS deployments in corporate environments

To get an accurate measurement of your knowledge, it is recommended that you give yourself 90 minutes to complete all 90 questions (or break it down into blocks of 30 questions per 30 minutes).

# Mock Exam Questions

1.  You are troubleshooting a smartphone that is exhibiting unusual behavior, such as slow performance, excessive data usage, and unexpected pop-up ads. You suspect it may be infected with malware. What is the most appropriate first step to take in addressing this issue? 
    1.  Perform a factory reset on the smartphone
    2.  Download a popular antivirus app and run a full scan
    3.  Disconnect the smartphone from the internet
    4.  Update the smartphone’s operating system and all apps
2.  Management within a banking organization is reviewing change management documentation. The team needs to understand the correct steps used within the process. When a change has been approved, what is the next step? 
    1.  End user acceptance
    2.  Conduct risk analysis
    3.  Communicate the change to stakeholders
    4.  Perform sandbox testing
3.  A user reports encountering sporadic, seemingly non-malicious advertisement notifications within the Windows 10 Action Center. These notifications suggest that the advertisements originate from a web browser. What is the MOST effective solution for a technician to implement? 
    1.  Deactivate the browser’s ability to send notifications to the Action Center
    2.  Perform a comprehensive antivirus scan on the computer
    3.  Turn off all notifications within the Action Center
    4.  Transfer specific site notifications from the Allowed category to Block
4.  What action should the systems administrator take to minimize the number of help desk tickets submitted, as reported by the manager regarding staff members frequently forgetting passwords for their mobile devices and applications? 
    1.  Enable multifactor authentication
    2.  Raise the failed login threshold
    3.  Eliminate complex password requirements
    4.  Implement a single sign-on system with biometrics
5.  A service desk technician is responding to a service ticket that appears to indicate the presence of a rootkit. In order to resolve the suspected rootkit installation, which of the following options would be the MOST effective? 
    1.  Updating applications
    2.  Utilizing anti-malware software
    3.  Reinstalling the operating system
    4.  Restoring files
6.  A technician is asked to re-deploy unused wireless access points for the new warehouse. The devices have been in storage since 2015. The technician must ensure they are configured with the most secure wireless network settings. Bearing in mind the age of the equipment, which configuration will provide the technician with the most secure implementation? 
    1.  WPA3 with AES-128
    2.  WPA2 with AES
    3.  WPA3 with AES-256
    4.  WPA2 with TKIP
7.  A call center technician receives a call from a home user seeking guidance on updating Windows. They would like to ensure that they are running the latest Windows edition when it is made available to customers. What is the appropriate course of action for the technician to take? 
    1.  Suggest that the user consider using a different system if they are unable to complete updates
    2.  Instruct the user to send their password via text to the technician
    3.  Guide the user to click on the Search field, type “Check for Updates,” and then press the Enter key
    4.  Suggest the user waits for an upcoming, automatic patch
8.  When a customer contacts a technician to report an issue, what practice should the technician employ to ensure the service ticket is correctly associated with the user? 
    1.  Verify the customer’s identity then issue a unique ticket
    2.  Assign a random ticket number to maintain customer privacy
    3.  Use the technician’s personal identification as the ticket reference
    4.  Create a generic ticket without associating it with any user
9.  The service desk for a large financial institution has raised support tickets after several members of staff returned to the workplace, after months of working remotely from home. When the users attempt to connect to the wired network, they encounter login errors. What is the most likely cause of the login issue for the users, considering that the network uses 802.1X with EAP-TLS for wired network authentication? 
    1.  The OS may need the latest security updates
    2.  An important service has not started
    3.  Application crash
    4.  The client certificates may have expired
10.  A user wants to exclude certain folders from the Windows search feature to improve search results. Which of the following steps should they follow? 
     1.  Disable Windows Search Service in Services.msc
     2.  Add the folders to the “Excluded Folders” list in the Windows search settings
     3.  Delete the folders from the hard drive
     4.  Reinstall the Windows operating system
11.  When the “cat ssh\_config” command is executed on a Linux terminal, which of the following outcomes should be anticipated? 
     1.  The original “ssh\_config” file will be replaced with a new blank document
     2.  The contents of the “ssh\_config” file will be displayed
     3.  The lines of text in the “ssh\_config” file will be sorted in alphabetical order
     4.  The contents of the “ssh\_config” file will be copied to another file named “ ssh\_config”
12.  In order to preserve evidence for potential litigation, what is the MOST likely action an incident handler will take? 
     1.  Apply encryption to the files
     2.  Create duplicates of any affected hard drives
     3.  Reach out to the cyber insurance company
     4.  Notify law enforcement authorities
13.  Important business data must be backed up to meet industry regulations, a technician is seeking the most suitable backup method. The requirement is to regularly modify only a few files while considering limited storage space. Which backup methods should the technician choose? 
     1.  Full backups
     2.  Incremental backups
     3.  Cloud-based backups
     4.  Tape rotation
14.  Why would a support technician set their mobile to silent before visiting a customer? 
     1.  To avoid disrupting the customer’s environment
     2.  To conserve battery life during the visit
     3.  To prevent incoming calls from being recorded
     4.  To enable location tracking for service records
15.  A technician must identify a solution to copy a large number of files, which may take several hours. In the past, they have experienced interrupted connections and network disruptions. If the network connection between the source and destination machines is unstable or experiences a temporary interruption, what will allow the file copying process to recover? 
     1.  System File Checker (sfc)
     2.  Check Disk (chkdsk)
     3.  Git Clone (git clone)
     4.  Robocopy
16.  A technician, working for a large regional bank, is configuring a new tape backup system. After installing the system in the server room, which backup types will need to be completed FIRST? 
     1.  Copy backup
     2.  Mirror backup
     3.  Differential backup
     4.  Full backup
17.  A user is experiencing frequent popups on their web browser. What will offer the BEST solution to remediate this problem? (Choose two.) 
     1.  Install a reputable ad blocker extension
     2.  Update the browser to the latest version
     3.  Disable the computer’s firewall
     4.  Ignore the popups; they are harmless
     5.  Download and run a registry cleaner tool
     6.  Reset the computer to factory settings
18.  When installing new software on a macOS computer, which of the following file extensions is most commonly used by technicians? 
     1.  .pkg
     2.  .bat
     3.  .msi
     4.  .dmg
19.  A user reports that their smartphone is overheating when not being used, and newly launched applications frequently crash. Which actions will BEST resolve these issues with minimal impact on the user? (Choose two.) 
     1.  Clear the app cache and data, update the apps, and ensure proper ventilation
     2.  Perform a factory reset and install a third-party cooling app
     3.  Disable automatic updates and limit app background processes
     4.  Buy a new smartphone with better hardware capabilities
     5.  Ignore the issues, as they are common with older smartphones
     6.  Close down unneeded applications
20.  A user’s desktop computer was automatically rebooted overnight, and now the system will no longer run some of the Windows applications. What is the recommended action to fix the problem? 
     1.  Perform a System Restore to a previous working state
     2.  Reinstall Windows
     3.  Delete the affected applications and reinstall them
     4.  Ignore the issue; it will resolve itself eventually
21.  What option from the list below can be utilized to establish secure physical access to a data center? 
     1.  Perimeter barrier
     2.  Intrusion detection system
     3.  Proximity card reader
     4.  Surveillance camera
22.  A user notifies a technician about a sluggish computer performance. Which of the following tools can assist the technician in identifying the underlying cause of the issue? 
     1.  System Optimizer
     2.  Performance Monitor
     3.  Registry Editor
     4.  Task Scheduler
23.  An open source software build is downloaded from the GitHub repository. Prior to installing the software image, a technician encounters the following string: 
     1.  7d3bb8e188cd9154e99b7ea8db38a24181efb2d1
     2.  Which option MOST accurately describes the intended use of this string?
     3.  Checksum for verification of download integrity
     4.  Encryption key
     5.  Product license key

24.  A user has installed unapproved utilities and applications on a mobile phone. The company has recently deployed MDM tools and would like to prevent users from sideloading unapproved applications. What two MDM policies will prevent future users from sideloading unapproved applications? 
     1.  App whitelisting
     2.  Enforce application store restrictions
     3.  Enable USB debugging control
     4.  Implement full device encryption
     5.  Enable remote wipe
     6.  Implement geofencing
25.  A technician is experiencing problems while trying to add a sales manager’s personal laptop to the company’s Active Directory domain. What is the most probable reason why a technician is unable to join a Windows 10 laptop computer to an Active Directory domain? 
     1.  The computer processor is an AMD version
     2.  The laptop is equipped with Windows 10 Home edition
     3.  The laptop does not have a touchscreen
     4.  The laptop is connected to a docking station
26.  An engineering firm is considering implementing the latest version of its computer-aided design (CAD) software, which includes a feature requiring backups of all CAD files to be stored externally in the software vendor’s data center. Which of the following is MOST likely to raise concerns for the IT manager? 
     1.  Comprehensive compatibility testing with all system types and accessories must be conducted for the updated software
     2.  Adequate budget allocation for additional staff hours during the installation of updates needs to be ensured
     3.  Network utilization is expected to significantly increase due to the larger size of CAD files being backed up

27.  A company employee receives a phone call from an individual falsely claiming to represent a mortgage company. Which of the following best describes this incident? 
     1.  Social engineering
     2.  Phishing
     3.  Impersonation
     4.  Identity theft
28.  Following an overnight network breach, security logs reveal that a dictionary attack successfully breached a single user’s account after 400 attempts. Which of the following measures would be the MOST effective in mitigating this specific threat? 
     1.  Two-factor authentication
     2.  Intrusion detection system
     3.  Password complexity requirements
     4.  Network segmentation
     5.  Account lockout
29.  A payroll manager has created a file on a shared drive and wants to protect it from accidental deletion by other team members. Which of the following applications should the technician recommend to assist the manager in hiding the file? 
     1.  Disk Cleanup
     2.  Task Scheduler
     3.  File Properties
     4.  Control Panel
30.  After dedicating several hours to troubleshooting a computer problem for the company’s Chief Executive Officer (CEO), the technician is now pressed for time to resolve the issue promptly. What should the technician do as the next step? 
     1.  Engage in further research to address the problem
     2.  Restart the diagnostic process from the beginning
     3.  Notify the CEO that the repair may take a few weeks
     4.  Escalate the support ticket to higher-level technicians
31.  While configuring a Small Office/Home Office (SOHO) device, a technician needs to ensure that the server maintains a consistent IP address at all times, following the company’s policy against static IP addresses. Which of the following options should the technician choose to achieve this requirement? 
     1.  IP address reservation via DHCP
     2.  Network address translation (NAT)
     3.  Dynamic Name System (DNS) record
     4.  Virtual private network (VPN) configuration
32.  A research scientist needs access to a specialist application that is only supported on a Linux OS. The scientist currently only has access to a Windows OS. The scientist needs to work in a remote laboratory with no access to external networks during the day. What offers the BEST solution for the scientist to access the specialist application? 
     1.  Pay for a remote desktop utilizing a Linux OS
     2.  Install the Linux application within the supported Windows Linux subsystem
     3.  Install Linux as the primary OS and purchase a second computer to handle the Windows application
     4.  Enable the Windows Hyper-V client and install the Linux OS and the Linux application

The system upgrades will involve replacing the processor and increasing the memory in the desktop computers. Which of the following should be done to ensure no damage occurs during this upgrade process? 
     1.  Ground yourself by using an anti-static wrist strap before handling any components
     2.  Upgrade the processor first, then add the memory modules afterward
     3.  Keep the computers powered on while making the upgrades
     4.  Skip the anti-static precautions as they are not necessary for desktop upgrades
34.  A customer calls the service desk as they would like to activate speech recognition on their Windows 10 Home PC. Which Windows Settings tool should the user utilize to enable this feature? 
     1.  Regional Settings
     2.  Accessibility
     3.  Appearance
     4.  Privacy
35.  A recent security audit within a company has identified the widespread use of several unauthorized software applications on employee’s work desktops, employees are engaged in social media activities using these installed applications. These activities are against the company’s policies. In light of this violation, which of the following should be modified to reflect this new requirement? 
     1.  EMM
     2.  SLA
     3.  DLP
     4.  AUP
36.  A departmental manager is proposing to upgrade the design team’s workstations with 32 GB of RAM. The plan is also to upgrade the OS from 32-bit to 64-bit. What will be the major benefit of the move to a 64-bit OS? 
     1.  A 64-bit OS can utilize more RAM efficiently, allowing applications to access larger amounts of memory
     2.  A 64-bit OS provides faster processing speeds than a 32-bit OS
     3.  A 64-bit OS improves graphics performance and rendering capabilities
     4.  A 64-bit OS enhances network connectivity and data transfer rates
37.  What is the most probable cause of an issue where a user cannot log in to the domain with a desktop PC, while a laptop PC on the same network functions properly, and a technician is unable to access the secure intranet site for troubleshooting tools? 
     1.  Network connectivity issues
     2.  Incorrect domain credentials
     3.  Browser compatibility problems
     4.  Firewall restrictions
38.  A network technician is deploying a new desktop computer into a SOHO environment. The computer must be configured so that the user can share folders with other Windows users on the network. Which of the following configurations should the technician implement to accomplish this objective?
